# System variables

Generated from `packages/sysvars/src/catalog/*.json` by `pnpm docs:sysvars`.

The catalog contains 860 variables: 798 documented fixed/metric defaults and 62 explicit environment/document-dependent initial values. Metadata was checked against Autodesk AutoCAD 2026 Windows help on 2026-10-15.

Registry/User-settings map to profile scope; Not-saved maps to session scope. Switch/Bitcode/Enum values are stored as integers, and Single-character values use their character code. Registration validates and stores values; it does not implement the feature controlled by that variable. Numeric constraints marked pending remain a parity gap.

HANDSEED and UNDOCTL come from the live database. Read-only runtime providers may supply document-specific values; unresolved initial values raise an explicit localized error until a valid stored/provider value is supplied. No environment-dependent value is replaced with an invented zero or path.

Metric defaults include INSUNITS=4, MEASUREMENT=1, ISO-25, LIMITS 420,297 and SNAPUNIT/GRIDUNIT 10,10. FONTALT and FONTMAP contain reference names only; no Autodesk resource files are bundled. DIMSTYLE is read-only; ISAVEBAK is the canonical backup variable. INPUTSEARCHOPTIONS is a command and is not registered as a sysvar.

## Bit meanings

| Name                    | Bit   | Meaning                                                                                                                  |
| ----------------------- | ----- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| 3DOSMODE                | 1     | Suppresses the current running 3D object snaps                                                                           |
| 3DOSMODE                | 2     | Snaps to a vertex or a control vertex                                                                                    |
| 3DOSMODE                | 4     | Snaps to the midpoint on a face edge                                                                                     |
| 3DOSMODE                | 8     | Snaps to the center of a face                                                                                            |
| 3DOSMODE                | 16    | Snaps to a spline or surface knot                                                                                        |
| 3DOSMODE                | 32    | Snaps to a perpendicular face (planar faces only)                                                                        |
| 3DOSMODE                | 64    | Snaps to an object nearest to a face                                                                                     |
| 3DOSMODE                | 128   | Snaps to the closest point (node) in point cloud                                                                         |
| 3DOSMODE                | 256   | Snaps to the nearest point on a planar segment of a point cloud                                                          |
| 3DOSMODE                | 512   | Snaps perpendicular to a planar segment of a point cloud                                                                 |
| 3DOSMODE                | 1024  | Snaps to the intersection of inferred lines for a sectioned point cloud, including extrapolated intersections            |
| 3DOSMODE                | 2048  | Snaps to the inferred edge of two planar segments in a point cloud                                                       |
| 3DOSMODE                | 4096  | Snaps perpendicular to the inferred edge of two planar segments in a point cloud                                         |
| 3DOSMODE                | 8192  | Snaps to the centerline of a cylindrical segment in a point cloud                                                        |
| 3DOSMODE                | 16384 | Snaps to the inferred intersection (corner) of three planar segments in a point cloud                                    |
| ACTIVITYINSIGHTSSUPPORT | 0     | Disables Activity Insights. The palette and all access points within the ribbon and command line                         |
| ACTIVITYINSIGHTSSUPPORT | 1     | Enables Activity Insights. Turns on the Activity Insights palette and displays Version History from Autodesk             |
| ACTIVITYINSIGHTSSUPPORT | 2     | Enables Activity Insights. Turns on the Activity Insights palette and displays Activity Insights activities. Note:       |
| ACTUI                   | 0     | No changes to the Action Recorder panel occur when recording or playing back an action                                   |
| ACTUI                   | 1     | Action Recorder panel is expanded during playback                                                                        |
| ACTUI                   | 2     | Action Recorder panel is expanded during recording                                                                       |
| ACTUI                   | 4     | When recording is finished, prompt for a name and description for the action macro                                       |
| AFLAGS                  | 0     | No attribute mode selected                                                                                               |
| AFLAGS                  | 1     | Invisible                                                                                                                |
| AFLAGS                  | 2     | Constant                                                                                                                 |
| AFLAGS                  | 4     | Verify                                                                                                                   |
| AFLAGS                  | 8     | Preset                                                                                                                   |
| AFLAGS                  | 16    | Lock position in block                                                                                                   |
| AFLAGS                  | 32    | Multiple lines                                                                                                           |
| APPAUTOLOAD             | 0     | Do not load plug-in applications at any time                                                                             |
| APPAUTOLOAD             | 1     | Display all messages when loading plug-in applications                                                                   |
| APPAUTOLOAD             | 2     | Load plug-in applications at startup                                                                                     |
| APPAUTOLOAD             | 4     | Load plug-in applications when a new drawing is opened                                                                   |
| APPAUTOLOAD             | 8     | Load plug-in applications when they appear in the plug-ins folder                                                        |
| AUTOSNAP                | 0     | Turns off the AutoSnap marker, tooltips, and magnet. Also turns off polar tracking, object snap                          |
| AUTOSNAP                | 1     | Turns on the AutoSnap marker                                                                                             |
| AUTOSNAP                | 2     | Turns on the AutoSnap tooltips                                                                                           |
| AUTOSNAP                | 4     | Turns on the AutoSnap magnet                                                                                             |
| AUTOSNAP                | 8     | Turns on polar tracking                                                                                                  |
| AUTOSNAP                | 16    | Turns on object snap tracking                                                                                            |
| AUTOSNAP                | 32    | Turns on tooltips for polar tracking, object snap tracking, and Ortho mode                                               |
| CMDACTIVE               | 0     | No active command                                                                                                        |
| CMDACTIVE               | 1     | Ordinary command is active                                                                                               |
| CMDACTIVE               | 2     | Transparent command is active                                                                                            |
| CMDACTIVE               | 4     | Script is active                                                                                                         |
| CMDACTIVE               | 8     | Dialog box is active                                                                                                     |
| CMDACTIVE               | 16    | Dynamic data exchange (DDE) is active                                                                                    |
| CMDACTIVE               | 32    | AutoLISP is active (only visible to an ObjectARX-defined command)                                                        |
| CMDACTIVE               | 64    | ObjectARX command is active                                                                                              |
| COMPAREPROPS            | 0     | Object property changes are not included in the drawing comparison                                                       |
| COMPAREPROPS            | 1     | Color                                                                                                                    |
| COMPAREPROPS            | 2     | Layer                                                                                                                    |
| COMPAREPROPS            | 4     | Linetype                                                                                                                 |
| COMPAREPROPS            | 8     | Linetype scale                                                                                                           |
| COMPAREPROPS            | 16    | Lineweight                                                                                                               |
| COMPAREPROPS            | 32    | Transparency                                                                                                             |
| COMPAREPROPS            | 64    | Thickness                                                                                                                |
| CONSTRAINTBARDISPLAY    | 0     | Automatically hides constraint bars after applying geometric constraints. Note: Constraint bars will always be displayed |
| CONSTRAINTBARDISPLAY    | 1     | Displays constraint bars as objects are geometrically constrained.                                                       |
| CONSTRAINTBARDISPLAY    | 2     | Displays any hidden constraints, both geometric and dimensional, but only as long as the constrained                     |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 1     | Horizontal                                                                                                               |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 2     | Vertical                                                                                                                 |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 4     | Perpendicular                                                                                                            |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 8     | Parallel                                                                                                                 |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 16    | Tangent                                                                                                                  |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 32    | Smooth                                                                                                                   |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 64    | Coincident                                                                                                               |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 128   | Concentric                                                                                                               |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 256   | Collinear                                                                                                                |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 512   | Symmetric                                                                                                                |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 1024  | Equal                                                                                                                    |
| CONSTRAINTBARMODE       | 2048  | Fix                                                                                                                      |
| DIMCONSTRAINTICON       | 0     | Does not display the lock icon next to the text for any dimensional constraints, either                                  |
| DIMCONSTRAINTICON       | 1     | Displays the icon for dynamic constraints                                                                                |
| DIMCONSTRAINTICON       | 2     | Displays the icon for annotational constraints                                                                           |
| DXEVAL                  | 0     | No notification                                                                                                          |
| DXEVAL                  | 1     | Open                                                                                                                     |
| DXEVAL                  | 2     | Save                                                                                                                     |
| DXEVAL                  | 4     | Plot                                                                                                                     |
| DXEVAL                  | 8     | Publish                                                                                                                  |
| DXEVAL                  | 16    | eTransmit/Archive                                                                                                        |
| DXEVAL                  | 32    | Save with Automatic Update                                                                                               |
| DXEVAL                  | 64    | Plot with Automatic Update                                                                                               |
| DXEVAL                  | 128   | Publish with Automatic Update                                                                                            |
| DXEVAL                  | 256   | eTransmit/Archive with Automatic Update                                                                                  |
| FIELDEVAL               | 0     | Not updated                                                                                                              |
| FIELDEVAL               | 1     | Updated on open                                                                                                          |
| FIELDEVAL               | 2     | Updated on save                                                                                                          |
| FIELDEVAL               | 4     | Updated on plot                                                                                                          |
| FIELDEVAL               | 8     | Updated on use of ETRANSMIT                                                                                              |
| FIELDEVAL               | 16    | Updated on regeneration                                                                                                  |
| GRIDDISPLAY             | 0     | Restricts the grid to the area specified by the LIMITS command                                                           |
| GRIDDISPLAY             | 1     | Does not restrict the grid to the area specified by the LIMITS command                                                   |
| GRIDDISPLAY             | 2     | Turns on adaptive grid display, which limits the density of the grid when zoomed out                                     |
| GRIDDISPLAY             | 4     | If the grid is set to adaptive display and when zoomed in, generates additional, more                                    |
| GRIDDISPLAY             | 8     | Changes the grid plane to follow the XY plane of the dynamic UCS. (Not applicable                                        |
| GRIDSTYLE               | 0     | Displays the lined grid for model space, the Block Editor, sheets, and paper space layouts.                              |
| GRIDSTYLE               | 1     | Displays the dotted grid for model space.                                                                                |
| GRIDSTYLE               | 2     | Displays the dotted grid for the Block Editor.                                                                           |
| GRIDSTYLE               | 4     | Displays the dotted grid for sheets and paper space layouts.                                                             |
| GRIPSUBOBJMODE          | 0     | Grips are NOT made hot when subobjects are selected.                                                                     |
| GRIPSUBOBJMODE          | 1     | Grips for the attributes of blocks, and the grips for the faces, edges, vertices, and                                    |
| GRIPSUBOBJMODE          | 2     | Grips for polyline segments are made hot when these subobjects are selected.                                             |
| INPUTHISTORYMODE        | 0     | No history of recent input is displayed.                                                                                 |
| INPUTHISTORYMODE        | 1     | History of recent input is displayed at the command line or in a dynamic prompt                                          |
| INPUTHISTORYMODE        | 2     | History of recent input for the current command is displayed in the shortcut menu under                                  |
| INPUTHISTORYMODE        | 4     | History of recent input for all commands in the current session is displayed in the                                      |
| INPUTHISTORYMODE        | 8     | Markers for recent input of point locations are displayed. Use the arrow keys at the                                     |
| LAYERNOTIFY             | 0     | Off                                                                                                                      |
| LAYERNOTIFY             | 1     | Plot                                                                                                                     |
| LAYERNOTIFY             | 2     | Open                                                                                                                     |
| LAYERNOTIFY             | 4     | Load/Reload/Attach for xrefs                                                                                             |
| LAYERNOTIFY             | 8     | Restore layer state                                                                                                      |
| LAYERNOTIFY             | 16    | Save                                                                                                                     |
| LAYERNOTIFY             | 32    | Insert                                                                                                                   |
| LOCKUI                  | 0     | Toolbars, panels, and windows not locked                                                                                 |
| LOCKUI                  | 1     | Docked toolbars and panels locked                                                                                        |
| LOCKUI                  | 2     | Docked or anchored windows locked                                                                                        |
| LOCKUI                  | 4     | Floating toolbars and panels locked                                                                                      |
| LOCKUI                  | 8     | Floating windows locked                                                                                                  |

| LOFTPARAM               | 8     | Close (closes the surface or solid between the first and the last cross sections)                                        |

| MENUECHO                | 4     | Disables ^P toggle of menu echoing                                                                                       |
| MENUECHO                | 8     | Displays input/output strings; debugging aid for DIESEL macros                                                           |

| PDFIMPORTMODE           | 0     | Does not process objects after they are imported. This includes converting Bezier curves to arcs,                        |
| PDFIMPORTMODE           | 1     | Combines imported objects into a block.                                                                                  |
| PDFIMPORTMODE           | 2     | Applies lineweight properties.                                                                                           |
| PDFIMPORTMODE           | 4     | Joins contiguous line and arc segments into polylines, when possible.                                                    |
| PDFIMPORTMODE           | 8     | Converts fills into solid-filled hatches rather than 2D solids.                                                          |
| PDFIMPORTMODE           | 16    | Combines sets of collinear segments into polyline segments.                                                              |
| PICKAUTO                | 0     | Turns off automatic window and crossing selection.                                                                       |

| PICKAUTO                | 4     | Begins a window or crossing lasso selection if the cursor is not directly over an                                        |
| POLARMODE               | 0     | Acquire automatically                                                                                                    |

## DXF header schema coverage

All 214 expanded header names from the [Autodesk 2026 HEADER table](https://help.autodesk.com/cloudhelp/2026/ENU/AutoCAD-DXF/files/GUID-A85E8E67-27CD-4C59-BE61-4DC9FADBE74A.htm) are represented. 170 have a matching registered command-variable identity. The other 44 are retained as header metadata instead of inventing SETVAR names or defaults.
